# Attribution - sakuhart dog tile pack

4254 photographs from the **Open Images V7** dataset (Google), every one
licensed **CC BY 2.0** (https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by/2.0/).

Each was cropped to a square holding the whole box drawn around the
dog -- never cutting into it -- and resized to 120px. The box
is at least 25% of the photograph's short side. Nothing else was changed.

CC BY 2.0 asks for credit to the photographer when an image is redistributed.
**If you publish a mosaic made from these photos, this file goes with it.**

- Photographs: **4254**
- Photographers: **2041**
